# Berean themes: contrast table

Generated by `contrast.py` from `tokens.json`. WCAG 2.x relative-luminance contrast; CIEDE2000 for highlight separation; Machado et al. 2009 (severity 1.0, linear sRGB) for colour-vision simulation. PASS/FAIL lines are hard floors; INFO/NOTE lines are informational (decorative separators, all-pairs ΔE, CVD).

**Result: ALL PASS** — 2448 checks, 2160 pass, 0 fail, 288 informational.

## Colour-vision notes: highlight pairs under ΔE2000 8 when simulated

Highlights carry no meaning (they are spoken by hue name, never used to encode state), so this cannot break WCAG 1.4.1. Listed so the picker can avoid placing these pairs side by side.
